[decision] 2026-04-18 YooAsset serialization fact-check: YooAsset uses custom binary stream (ManifestTools.SerializeToBinary), NOT MessagePack. FileSign+FileVersion header, count-prefixed entries. JSON via JsonUtility for debug only
[decision] 2026-04-18 Binary codec technical route: zero-dependency custom binary + editor code generator. [BinarySerializable]/[BinaryField(n)] attributes on data classes, editor tool scans and generates Serializer classes. Eliminates "change field = change two places" maintenance cost while keeping zero external dependencies
[decision] 2026-04-18 Rejected alternatives: (1) MessagePack — developer prefers zero dependency. (2) MemoryPack — Unity 2022.3 Source Generator compatibility risk. (3) Pure handwritten binary (YooAsset-style) — high maintenance cost during manifest evolution phase
[decision] 2026-04-18 Binary header design: lightweight 8-byte fixed header (Magic uint32 + SchemaVersion uint16 + Flags uint16). Per-type independent Magic values (ABManifest='ABMF', etc). Codec determined by file extension (.json/.bin), header for format self-identification and version validation
[decision] 2026-04-18 Schema evolution rules: new fields append at end with incrementing BinaryField number; deleted fields marked [Obsolete] keeping number slot; SchemaVersion increments for field set changes; incompatible changes require major version jump + migration tool
[decision] 2026-04-18 Auto format detection: SerializationUtility.Deserialize<T>(byte[]) checks first 4 bytes against registered Magic → binary codec; no match → JSON fallback. Enables new client reading old JSON files seamlessly
[decision] 2026-04-18 Old backend artifacts (version_state.json, BuildIndex.json) not binary-ized — natural retirement with legacy backend. Only need version mismatch detection to block incompatible reads
[decision] 2026-04-18 BuildIndex.json correction: enters runtime via StreamingAssets (not editor-only), but still not binary-ized as legacy backend artifact
[decision] 2026-04-18 Serialization 4-phase roadmap: S1 (interface+JsonCodec) → S2 (BinaryCodec+code generator) → S3 (ABManifest binary) → S4 (runtime integration+compat layer)
[decision] 2026-04-18 S1 file placement: Utility/Serialization/ as general-purpose infrastructure (same level as HashGenerator/PathManager)
[decision] 2026-04-18 S1 replacement strategy: all 10 JsonUtility call sites replaced in one batch (ABManifest.cs, ManifestLoader.cs, HotfixManager.cs, BuildProjectManager.cs, LocalStatusExporter.cs)
[decision] 2026-04-18 S1 interface design confirmed: ISerializationCodec (byte[]<->T only), JsonCodec (wraps JsonUtility), SerializationUtility (facade: codec registry + format detection + sync/async file I/O), BinaryHeader (Magic registry + HasValidMagic, inactive in S1)
[decision] 2026-04-18 Developer noted HotfixManager should be refactored to new/old backend split (like AssetPackageManager) — separate plan, can be done before or after S1
